\frac{d}{dt}\bigg(t^{6\mathfrak{q}}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{p}}F\log Fdxdp\bigg)\\
=6\mathfrak{q}t^{6\mathfrak{q}-1}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{p}}F\log Fdxdp+t^{6\mathfrak{q}}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{p}}\partial_{t}F(1+\log F)dxdp\\
=6\mathfrak{q}t^{6\mathfrak{q}-1}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{p}}(F\log F)dxdp\\
-t^{6\mathfrak{q}}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{p}}\Big[\frac{p^{i}}{|t^{\mathfrak{q}}p|}\partial_{x^{i}}F-\frac{2\mathfrak{q}}{t}p^{i}\partial_{p^{i}}F-Q(F,F)\Big](1+\log F)dxdp\\
=t^{6\mathfrak{q}}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{p}}Q(F,F)\log Fdxdp\leq 0.

This completes the proof.
∎

Appendix B
Determination of the Maxwell–Jüttner parameters

In this section, the Maxwell–Jüttner parameters are identified for fixed values of the initial mass, energy, and momentum.

Theorem B.1

(Determination of Maxwell–Jüttner parameters from conserved quantities)
.

Given a sufficiently regular initial distribution
F_{1}\colon\mathbb{T}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{p}\to[0,+\infty)
, there are parameters
a\in\mathbb{R}
,
c\in[0,+\infty)
, and
b\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
such that
c>|b|
, for which

J_{a,b,c}(p)=(8\pi)^{-1}e^{a+b\cdot p-c|p|}

satisfies

0=\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}_{x}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{p}}(F_{1}-J_{a,b,c})dpdx=\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}_{x}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{p}}p^{i}(F_{1}-J_{a,b,c})dpdx\\
=\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}_{x}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{p}}|p|(F_{1}-J_{a,b,c})dpdx.

The proof of Theorem
B.1
follows from the propositions obtained below.

Proposition B.2

(Mass, momentum and energy of the Maxwell–Jüttner equilibria)
.

Let
a
,
c\in\mathbb{R}
, and
b\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
such that
c>|b|
. The mass, momentum, and energy of the Maxwell–Jüttner equilibrium
J_{a,b,c}
are

\displaystyle t^{6\mathfrak{q}}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}_{x}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{p}}J_{a,b,c}(t^{{2\mathfrak{q}}}p)dpdx=\frac{e^{a}c}{(c^{2}-|b|^{2})^{2}},

\displaystyle t^{6\mathfrak{q}}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}_{x}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{p}}t^{2\mathfrak{q}}p^{i}J_{a,b,c}(t^{{2\mathfrak{q}}}p)dpdx=\frac{4e^{a}cb^{i}}{(c^{2}-|b|^{2})^{3}},

\displaystyle t^{6\mathfrak{q}}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}_{x}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{p}}|t^{2\mathfrak{q}}p|J_{a,b,c}(t^{{2\mathfrak{q}}}p)dpdx=e^{a}\frac{(3c^{2}+|b|^{2})}{(c^{2}-|b|^{2})^{3}}.

Proof.

First, we compute the mass of
J_{a,b,c}(t^{{2\mathfrak{q}}}p)
by making the change of variables
q=t^{2\mathfrak{q}}p
to get

\displaystyle t^{6\mathfrak{q}}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}_{x}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{p}}J_{a,b,c}(t^{{2\mathfrak{q}}}p)dpdx

\displaystyle=\frac{t^{6q}e^{a}}{8\pi}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{p}}e^{b\cdot(t^{2\mathfrak{q}}p)-c|t^{2\mathfrak{q}}p|}dp

\displaystyle=\frac{e^{a}}{8\pi}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{q}}e^{b\cdot q-c|q|}dq=\frac{e^{a}c}{(c^{2}-|b|^{2})^{2}},

where the last integral was computed using spherical coordinates. The momentum and energy of
J_{a,b,c}(t^{{2\mathfrak{q}}}p)
are computed in a similar way.
∎

Then, we need to solve for
a
,
c\in\mathbb{R}
, and
b\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
in the following system of equations

(B.1)

\displaystyle A=\frac{e^{a}c}{(c^{2}-|b|^{2})^{2}},\qquad B^{i}=\frac{4e^{a}cb^{i}}{(c^{2}-|b|^{2})^{3}},\qquad C=e^{a}\frac{(3c^{2}+|b|^{2})}{(c^{2}-|b|^{2})^{3}}.

Proposition B.3
.

Let
A
,
C\in(0,+\infty)
, and
B\in[0,+\infty)^{3}
such that
C\geq|B|
. The nonlinear system of equations (
B.1
) has a unique solution
a\in\mathbb{R}
,
c\in(0,+\infty)
, and
b\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
such that
c>|b|
. In fact, we have

\displaystyle e^{a}

\displaystyle=\frac{864A^{4}}{(C+\Delta)(4(C+\Delta)^{2}-9|B|^{2})},\quad\qquad b^{i}=\frac{36AB^{i}}{4(C+\Delta)^{2}-9|B|^{2}},

\displaystyle c

\displaystyle=\frac{24A(C+\Delta)}{4(C+\Delta)^{2}-9|B|^{2}},\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad\Delta=\sqrt{C^{2}-\frac{3}{4}|B|^{2}}.

Proof.

By the condition
C\geq|B|
there holds
\Delta\geq\frac{1}{4}|B|^{2}\geq 0
. Moreover, we get from the equations (
B.1
) that

4(C+\Delta)^{2}-9|B|^{2}=\frac{144e^{a}c^{2}}{(c^{2}-|b|^{2})^{5}}>0.

Finally, the result can be shown by direct computations.
∎
